# Complete current context catalog

Verbatim stored formulations from the authorized independent workspace; no external source verification. Hashes identify inventory versions and effective replay nodes, not citation certification. Seed rows are separate from editable live-node occurrences. All captured meaning variants are indexed in `EVIDENCE.json/current_meaning_variants`. Historical source texts/edges and all original review hashes are retained in the inventory.

## `form.003cb1cc70cf5a2c.locally-nilpotent-ideal`

**Meaning:** `meaning.003cb1cc70cf5a2c.locally-nilpotent-ideal` — locally nilpotent (nil) ideal. Commutative algebra: locally nilpotent (nil) ideal

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
An ideal I in a commutative ring R is locally nilpotent (in the Stacks Project terminology) when each element x of I is nilpotent, with the exponent allowed to depend on x.
```

## `form.003cb1cc70cf5a2c.nilpotent-ideal`

**Meaning:** `meaning.003cb1cc70cf5a2c.nilpotent-ideal` — nilpotent ideal. Commutative algebra: nilpotent ideal

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
An ideal I of a ring R is nilpotent when there is a single positive integer n for which the product ideal I^n is the zero ideal.
```

Source version `522b853e8897f06f6e63b3d0a51885674f8b5332ce8aa65c1cf41744b37343ac`; source snapshot `0732efe74beb97ad595013d4abf253884ca7fa7a41545de293e69522c3fdc79e`; effective node `59042cf6b7e9f908d3e8fa3ce90044e3f8e4fa29d31ad708f4bdf7ad61bb2c0a`.

## `form.03b55b8ca41abb20.jacobson-ring`

**Meaning:** `meaning.03b55b8ca41abb20.jacobson-ring` — Jacobson ring. Commutative algebra: Jacobson ring

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
A commutative ring R is a Jacobson ring when every radical ideal I of R equals the intersection of the maximal ideals of R containing I.
```

**Meaning:** `meaning.03b55b8ca41abb20.jacobson-ring` — Jacobson ring. Commutative algebra: Jacobson ring

## `form.05531f966e3a206f.dedekind-domain`

**Meaning:** `meaning.05531f966e3a206f.dedekind-domain` — ideal-factorization characterization of Dedekind domain. Commutative algebra: ideal-factorization characterization of Dedekind domain

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
An integral domain R is a Dedekind domain in this factorization characterization if every nonzero ideal factors as a finite product of nonzero prime ideals and this decomposition is unique up to permuting the factors.
```

**Meaning:** `meaning.05531f966e3a206f.irreducible-element` — irreducible element. Factorization in integral domains: irreducible element

## `form.097db25e2f156689.clopen-subset-of-spectrum`

**Meaning:** `meaning.097db25e2f156689.clopen-subset-of-spectrum` — clopen subsets of an affine spectrum. Commutative algebra: clopen subsets of an affine spectrum

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
For a commutative ring R, a subset U of Spec(R) is simultaneously open and closed exactly when there is an idempotent e of R (so e²=e) such that U=D(e)={p in Spec(R):e is not in p}; this e is unique.
```

## `form.0c4c23579959165d.algebraic-independence`

**Meaning:** `meaning.0c4c23579959165d.algebraic-independence` — algebraic independence over a base field. Field theory: algebraic independence over a base field

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
A family (x_i) of elements of a field extension K/k is algebraically independent over k when the evaluation homomorphism k[X_i:i∈I]→K, sending each indeterminate X_i to x_i, is injective.
```

## `form.12e554f8d49d5361.purely-inseparable-extension`

**Meaning:** `meaning.12e554f8d49d5361.purely-inseparable-extension` — purely inseparable extension. Stacks Definition 9.14.1: extension in characteristic p>0, every element has a suitable p-power in the base field

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
For a field F of characteristic p greater than zero, an extension K/F is purely inseparable if, for every alpha in K, some p-power alpha^(p^n) lies in F.
```

## `form.138b13bf27c037c1.naive-cotangent-complex`

**Meaning:** `meaning.138b13bf27c037c1.naive-cotangent-complex` — naive cotangent complex of a ring homomorphism. Commutative algebra: naive cotangent complex of a ring homomorphism

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
For a ring map R→S, let P=R[X_s:s∈S] map canonically onto S and put I=ker(P→S). The naive cotangent complex NL_{S/R} is the two-term complex I/I²→Ω_{P/R}⊗_P S, with I/I² in homological degree 1 and the induced differential from universal derivation.
```

## `form.1be2fa074ef447b0.i-adic-completeness`

**Meaning:** `meaning.1be2fa074ef447b0.i-adic-completeness` — I-adically complete module or ring. Commutative algebra: I-adically complete module or ring

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
An R-module M is I-adically complete when its canonical map M→lim_n M/I^n M is an isomorphism; a ring R is I-adically complete when it has this property as its own module.
```

<a id="context-013"></a>
## `form.1be2fa074ef447b0.i-adic-module-completion`

**Meaning:** `meaning.1be2fa074ef447b0.i-adic-module-completion` — I-adic completion of a module. Commutative algebra: I-adic completion of a module

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
For an R-module M and ideal I⊆R, the I-adic completion of M is lim_n M/I^n M; its elements can be viewed as mutually compatible residue classes modulo powers of I.
```

## `form.1be2fa074ef447b0.i-adic-ring-completion`

**Meaning:** `meaning.1be2fa074ef447b0.i-adic-ring-completion` — I-adic completion of a ring. Commutative algebra: I-adic completion of a ring

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
For a commutative ring R and ideal I, its I-adic completion R^ is the inverse limit of the quotient rings R/I^n over positive integers n, with transition maps given by reduction modulo smaller powers.
```

**Meaning:** `meaning.22c049b8eee93897.krull-dimension-ring` — Krull dimension of a ring. Commutative algebra: Krull dimension of a ring

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
The Krull dimension of a commutative ring R is the supremum of the lengths of all finite strict chains of prime ideals in R, with the value infinite when those lengths are unbounded.
```

## `form.2cde0248ffe81c00.tor-functor-modules`

**Meaning:** `meaning.2cde0248ffe81c00.tor-functor-modules` — derived tensor/Tor module. Homological algebra: derived tensor/Tor module

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
For modules M and N over a commutative ring R and integer i≥0, Tor_i^R(M,N) is the ith homology module of F_•⊗_R N, where F_• is any free resolution of M; its isomorphism type is independent of that resolution.
```

```text
Given a field F, an algebraic closure of F is an extension field K containing F in which every element is algebraic over F and every nonconstant polynomial over K has a root in K.
```

**Meaning:** `meaning.39488f3e396a1c77.algebraically-closed` — algebraically closed. algebraically closed

## `form.445e8dc5147c408d.tensor-algebra`

**Meaning:** `meaning.445e8dc5147c408d.tensor-algebra` — tensor algebra. tensor algebra

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
For an R-module M, its tensor algebra is the graded associative R-algebra obtained by taking the direct sum over n at least zero of the n-fold tensor powers of M, with the degree-zero summand R and multiplication by concatenation of tensors.
```

## `form.44fae2299dfd2f0a.formally-unramified-map`

**Meaning:** `meaning.44fae2299dfd2f0a.formally-unramified-map` — formally unramified ring homomorphism. Commutative algebra: formally unramified ring homomorphism

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
A ring homomorphism R→S is formally unramified if, for every R-algebra A and square-zero ideal I⊆A, any R-algebra map S→A/I admits at most one lift to an R-algebra map S→A.
```

## `form.471f985f2fbe7b49.splitting-field`

**Meaning:** `meaning.471f985f2fbe7b49.splitting-field` — splitting field. splitting field



## `form.711d6a9a3f5a61ca.relative-de-rham-complex`

**Meaning:** `meaning.711d6a9a3f5a61ca.relative-de-rham-complex` — relative algebraic de Rham complex. Commutative algebra: relative algebraic de Rham complex

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
For a ring homomorphism A→B, the relative de Rham complex Ω^•_{B/A} is the graded complex with Ω^p_{B/A}=∧^p_B Ω^1_{B/A}, Ω^0=B, and differentials d:Ω^p→Ω^{p+1} uniquely extending the universal derivation and satisfying d(b_0 db_1∧⋯∧db_p)=db_0∧db_1∧⋯∧db_p.
```

## `form.720eaae9156f6f4e.separable-degree-polynomial`

## `form.8a5f1888f076f50d.formally-etale-ring-map`

**Meaning:** `meaning.8a5f1888f076f50d.formally-etale-ring-map` — formally étale ring map. Commutative algebra: formally étale ring map

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
A ring map R→S is formally étale if for every R-algebra A and ideal I⊆A with I²=0, every R-algebra map S→A/I lifts to exactly one R-algebra map S→A.
```

## `form.8f376cdea16a26da.closed-immersion-locally-ringed-space`

**Meaning:** `meaning.8f376cdea16a26da.closed-immersion-locally-ringed-space` — closed immersion of locally ringed spaces. Algebraic geometry: closed immersion of locally ringed spaces

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
A morphism i:Z→X of locally ringed spaces is a closed immersion if its underlying map identifies Z homeomorphically with a closed subset of X, the sheaf homomorphism O_X→i_*O_Z is surjective, and its kernel is locally generated by sections.
```

## `form.951ae8411d8a974b.depth-module-ideal`

**Meaning:** `meaning.951ae8411d8a974b.depth-module-ideal` — depth of a finite module relative to an ideal. Commutative algebra: depth of a finite module relative to an ideal

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
For a finite R-module M and ideal I⊆R, depth_I(M) is the supremum of lengths of M-regular sequences whose entries lie in I when IM≠M, and is defined to be infinite when IM=M.
```

## `form.988d26ef7c849694.epimorphism-commutative-rings`

**Meaning:** `meaning.988d26ef7c849694.epimorphism-commutative-rings` — categorical epimorphism of rings. Commutative algebra: categorical epimorphism of rings

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
A ring homomorphism f:R→S is an epimorphism in the category of commutative rings if every pair of ring maps g,h:S→T satisfying g∘f=h∘f are equal; equivalently the multiplication map S⊗_R S→S is an isomorphism.
```

**Meaning:** `meaning.a81a0fbec29f2186.finite-order-differential-operator` — differential operator of finite order. Commutative algebra: differential operator of finite order

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
For a ring map R→S and S-modules M,N, an R-linear map D:M→N is a differential operator of order at most k if k=0 and D is S-linear, or if k>0 and for every s∈S the commutator m↦D(sm)−sD(m) has order at most k−1.
```

## `form.ab260940ea9c523d.connected-topological-space`

**Meaning:** `meaning.ab260940ea9c523d.connected-topological-space` — connected space. Topology: connected space

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
A nonempty topological space X is connected if it cannot be expressed as a disjoint union of two nonempty open subsets; equivalently its only clopen subsets are ∅ and X.
```

Source version `bcf746fc860bc9ef39a5e948b15dc8733dac3445193931a92701d51f77c1b047`; source snapshot `f1b88ac9a5a91b08c2dae40f2a52971ec308d981354023a6d8d87bf7c548ebe4`; effective node `6559b79c290c155da4faea0f262a3941a3b77c63ebd739f2cd3c921343a17335`.

## `form.b3aefc22ee361c53.kummer-extension`

**Meaning:** `meaning.b3aefc22ee361c53.kummer-extension` — Kummer extension generated by an nth root. Field theory: Kummer extension generated by an nth root

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
In the Kummer setting, an extension L/K is obtained by adjoining an element α satisfying α^n=a∈K, where n≥2 is prime to char(K) and K contains the nth roots of unity; when [L:K] is finite, L/K is a cyclic Galois extension of degree dividing n.
```

## `form.bd90654fb341045a.krull-dimension-space`

**Meaning:** `meaning.bd90654fb341045a.krull-dimension-space` — Krull dimension of a topological space. Topology: Krull dimension of a topological space

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
The Krull dimension of a topological space X is the supremum of lengths of strict finite chains of irreducible closed subsets of X, taking dimension −∞ for the empty space.
```

## `form.be14669c52f30e79.categorical-fibre-product`

**Meaning:** `meaning.be14669c52f30e79.categorical-fibre-product` — pullback or fibre product. Category theory: pullback or fibre product

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
Given arrows f:X→S and g:Y→S in a category, a fibre product is an object P with arrows p:P→X and q:P→Y satisfying fp=gq, such that every pair u:T→X, v:T→Y with fu=gv factors uniquely through a map T→P.
```

## `form.c236facceae1dd0f.geometrically-regular-noetherian-algebra`

**Meaning:** `meaning.c236facceae1dd0f.geometrically-regular-noetherian-algebra` — geometrically regular Noetherian algebra. Commutative algebra: geometrically regular Noetherian algebra

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
A Noetherian k-algebra R is geometrically regular over a field k if R⊗_k K is a regular ring for every finite field extension K/k; equivalently this holds for all field extensions K/k under the hypotheses of the Stacks criterion.
```

## `form.cbe7158b0742af88.direct-system-modules`

**Meaning:** `meaning.cbe7158b0742af88.direct-system-modules` — direct system of modules. Module theory: direct system of modules

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
A system of R-modules indexed by a preordered set I consists of modules M_i and maps μ_ij:M_i→M_j whenever i≤j with μ_ii=id and μ_ik=μ_jk∘μ_ij; it is directed if I is directed.
```

**Meaning:** `meaning.d0a078e59e1495da.algebraic-element-extension` — algebraic element over a field. Field theory: algebraic element over a field

## `form.dbce785fa0c96b0a.field-characteristic`

**Meaning:** `meaning.dbce785fa0c96b0a.field-characteristic` — characteristic. Field theory: characteristic

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
The characteristic of a field F is the least positive integer n with n·1_F=0 when such an integer exists, and otherwise equals zero; a positive characteristic must be prime.
```

## `form.e39c701ba05418fb.formal-smoothness-field-extension`

**Meaning:** `meaning.e39c701ba05418fb.formal-smoothness-field-extension` — formal smoothness of a field extension. Field theory: formal smoothness of a field extension

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
A field extension K/k is formally smooth over k when every k-algebra map K→A/I across a square-zero quotient lifts to A. In positive characteristic this is equivalent to separability of K/k; in characteristic zero every field extension satisfies it.
```

**Meaning:** `meaning.f17ea18ebee0660f.module-regular-sequence` — regular sequence on a module. Commutative algebra: regular sequence on a module

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
A finite sequence f_1,…,f_r in a ring R is M-regular for an R-module M if f_i acts injectively on M/(f_1,…,f_{i-1})M for every i and the final quotient M/(f_1,…,f_r)M is nonzero.
```

## `form.fa3b484c09596cfd.graded-module`

**Meaning:** `meaning.fa3b484c09596cfd.graded-module` — graded module. Graded commutative algebra: graded module

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
A graded module M over S=⊕_{n≥0}S_n is an S-module with a direct-sum decomposition M=⊕_{j∈Z}M_j and S_nM_j⊆M_{n+j}.
```

**Meaning:** `meaning.fa3b484c09596cfd.nonnegatively-graded-ring` — nonnegative ring grading. Graded commutative algebra: nonnegative ring grading

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
A nonnegatively graded ring S is a direct sum S=⊕_{n≥0}S_n of additive groups satisfying S_nS_m⊆S_{n+m} for every n,m≥0.
```

## `form.wave2.alternative39.borel-sigma-algebra-topological`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.alternative39.borel-sigma-algebra-topological` — Borel sigma-algebra of a topological space. Topology and measure theory: smallest sigma-algebra containing every open subset of a topological space

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
Let (X,τ) be a topological space. The Borel sigma-algebra B(X,τ) is the smallest sigma-algebra on X containing τ, equivalently the intersection of all sigma-algebras on X containing every τ-open subset.
```

## `form.wave2.analysis.orthogonal-polynomials-measure`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.analysis.orthogonal-polynomials-measure` — orthogonal polynomial system. DLMF Lebesgue–Stieltjes measure with infinitely many points of increase

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
Let μ be a nonnegative Lebesgue–Stieltjes measure arising from an admissible bounded nondecreasing function with infinitely many points of increase and finite moments ∫|x|^k dμ(x)<∞ for all integers k≥0. A polynomial family p_n of degree n is orthogonal with respect to μ when ∫p_n(x)p_m(x)dμ(x)=0 for n≠m.
```

## `form.wave2.branch129.operator-eigenvalue.1`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.branch129.operator-eigenvalue` — eigenvalue of a field-linear operator. Eigenvalue of a K-linear endomorphism without finite-dimensionality assumption

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
For a K-vector space V and K-linear operator A:V→V, λ∈K is an eigenvalue of A if ker(A−λI_V)≠{0_V}, where I_V is the identity map of V.
```

## `form.wave2.found85.conditional-expectation`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.found85.conditional-expectation` — conditional expectation given a sigma-algebra. Conditional expectation with respect to a sub-sigma-algebra

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
For an integrable real random variable X on (Ω,F,P) and a sub-σ-algebra G⊆F, a version of E[X|G] is an integrable G-measurable random variable Z satisfying ∫_A Z dP = ∫_A X dP for every A∈G.
```

## `form.wave2.found85.discrete-filtration`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.found85.discrete-filtration` — filtration of sigma-algebras. Discrete time: increasing sub-sigma-algebras indexed by n≥0

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
For a measurable space (Ω,F), a discrete-time filtration is a family (F_n)_{n≥0} of sub-σ-algebras of F such that F_n⊆F_m whenever n≤m.
```

## `form.wave2.foundation.left-module-nonunital`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.foundation.left-module-nonunital` — left module over a ring, optionally nonunital. General left R-module over an associative ring; unitality a separate condition

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
Let R be an associative ring and (M,+) an abelian group. A left R-module structure on M is an action R×M→M, (r,m)↦rm, satisfying r(m+n)=rm+rn, (r+s)m=rm+sm, and (rs)m=r(sm). When R has identity, a unital left module additionally satisfies 1_R m=m for all m∈M.
```

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.foundation.nonnegative-measure` — nonnegative measure. Measure theory: extended nonnegative countably additive set function on a sigma-algebra

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
Let Σ be a sigma-algebra of subsets of X. A nonnegative measure on (X,Σ) is a function μ:Σ→[0,+∞] with μ(∅)=0 and μ(⋃_{n≥1}E_n)=∑_{n≥1}μ(E_n) for every sequence (E_n) of pairwise disjoint sets in Σ.
```

## `form.wave2.foundation.nonzero-polynomial-degree`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.foundation.nonzero-polynomial-degree` — degree of a nonzero univariate polynomial. Formal algebra: nonzero polynomial in R[X] over a commutative unital ring

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
Let R be a commutative ring with multiplicative identity and let P=∑_{k=0}^n a_kX^k∈R[X] be nonzero. Its degree deg(P) is the greatest integer k≥0 for which a_k≠0_R. This definition does not assign a finite degree to the zero polynomial.
```

## `form.wave2.foundation.ordered-pair-kuratowski`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.foundation.ordered-pair-kuratowski` — ordered pair. Set theory: Kuratowski realization with coordinatewise equality

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
For arbitrary sets a and b, the Kuratowski ordered pair is (a,b):={{a},{a,b}}. This set-theoretic construction has the defining property (a,b)=(c,d) if and only if a=c and b=d.
```

## `form.wave2.foundation.orthogonality-measure-raw-moment`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.foundation.orthogonality-measure-raw-moment` — nth raw moment of an orthogonality measure. DLMF orthogonal-polynomial analysis: raw power moment with respect to a positive measure

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
Let μ be a nonnegative Borel or Lebesgue–Stieltjes measure on a real interval (a,b) and let n be a nonnegative integer such that x↦x^n is μ-integrable. The nth raw moment of μ is μ_n=∫_a^b x^n dμ(x). It is finite only when the integral is finite.
```

## `form.wave2.foundation.reduced-ring`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.foundation.reduced-ring` — reduced ring. Algebra: associative ring, not necessarily unital, with no nonzero nilpotent elements

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
An associative ring R is reduced if for every r∈R and every positive integer n, the equality r^n=0 implies r=0. Equivalently, its only nilpotent element is zero.
```

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.foundation.sigma-algebra` — sigma-algebra. Measure theory: family of subsets closed under relative complements and countable unions

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
Let X be a set. A sigma-algebra on X is a family Σ⊆P(X) containing X, closed under taking complements X\A for A∈Σ, and closed under countable unions ⋃_{n≥1}A_n of members A_n∈Σ.
```

## `form.wave2.foundation.strongly-transcendental-subring-element`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.foundation.strongly-transcendental-subring-element` — strongly transcendental element over a subring. Stacks 10.123.7: strong transcendence under a commutative ring inclusion, with annihilators and potential zero divisors

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
Let R⊆S be an inclusion of commutative rings with identity, and let x∈S. The element x is strongly transcendental over R if for every integer n≥0, coefficients a_0,…,a_n∈R, and u∈S, the equation u(a_0+a_1x+⋯+a_nx^n)=0 implies ua_i=0 for every i=0,…,n.
```

## `form.wave2.foundation.subset-nonstrict`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.foundation.subset-nonstrict` — subset. Set theory: non-strict subset relation for sets

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
For sets A and B, A is a subset of B, written A⊆B, if and only if for every x, x∈A implies x∈B. Equality A=B is allowed in this non-strict inclusion relation.
```

## `form.wave2.foundation.subset-proper`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.foundation.subset-proper` — proper subset. Set theory: strict set inclusion requiring inequality

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
For sets A and B, A is a proper subset of B, written A⊊B, if and only if A⊆B and A≠B. Equivalently, every member of A belongs to B and at least one member of B does not belong to A.
```

## `form.wave2.foundation.tagged-riemann-integral`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.foundation.tagged-riemann-integral` — proper Riemann integral on a compact interval. Classical real analysis: universal sufficiently fine tagged partition integral

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
Let f:[a,b]→R be bounded with a<b. For a finite partition a=x_0<⋯<x_n=b and arbitrary tags ξ_i∈[x_{i−1},x_i], let S=Σ_{i=1}^n f(ξ_i)(x_i−x_{i−1}) and mesh=max_i(x_i−x_{i−1}). The function f is Riemann integrable on [a,b] with integral L∈R if for every ε>0 there exists δ>0 such that |S−L|<ε for every tagged partition with mesh<δ.
```

Source version `b72811bab9eb7f7ef523f412f6e92416751c8820e34b45fe730446e5178c1660`; source snapshot `2d8d2696eab4d6d854119231dba504e6d19e3393ccb23289b05a68762c5c19f6`; effective node `20153d4287058de9a30cfb9cd8839d58bf5326395df468183ac9550cf25fe57e`.

<a id="context-178"></a>
## `form.wave2.foundation.topological-space`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.foundation.topological-space` — topological space. Open-set axioms for arbitrary topological spaces, including the empty space

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
A topological space is a pair (X,τ) with τ⊆P(X) such that ∅∈τ and X∈τ, any union of members of τ is in τ, and intersections of finitely many members of τ are in τ. Members of τ are the open subsets of X.
```

## `form.wave2.foundation.two-sided-ideal`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.foundation.two-sided-ideal` — two-sided ideal of a ring. Two-sided ring ideal; multiplication need not be commutative

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
Let R be a ring. A subset I⊆R is a two-sided ideal if (I,+) is an additive subgroup of (R,+) and, for every r∈R and i∈I, both ri and ir belong to I.
```

## `form.wave2.foundation.unit-of-ring`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.foundation.unit-of-ring` — unit of a unital ring. Associative ring with 1: two-sided multiplicative invertibility; distinct from the unity 1_R

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
Let R be an associative ring with identity 1_R. An element u in R is a unit if there exists v in R such that uv=1_R=vu. The identity is required, but u itself need not equal 1_R.
```

## `form.wave2.foundation13.exact-sequence-abelian-category`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.foundation13.exact-sequence-abelian-category` — exact sequence. Complex exact at interior objects of an abelian category, where images and kernels exist

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
A sequence of arrows in an additive category is a complex when each consecutive composite is zero. When the category is abelian, a complex X→Y→Z is exact at Y if im(X→Y)=ker(Y→Z) as subobjects of Y. An exact complex is exact at every applicable position; a short exact sequence has form 0→A→B→C→0.
```

## `form.wave2.foundation76.continuous-topological-map.01`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.foundation76.continuous-topological-map` — continuous map. Mapping between topological spaces, equivalent global and pointwise forms

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
A map f:X→Y between topological spaces is continuous when, for every open subset V⊆Y, the inverse image f^{-1}(V) is open in X.
```

## `form.wave2.graph42.loopless-undirected-multigraph`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.graph42.loopless-undirected-multigraph` — loopless undirected multigraph. Multiset of unordered vertex doubletons allows parallel undirected edges but no loops; does not demand finite vertices

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
A loopless undirected multigraph is a pair G=(V,E), where V is a set of vertices and E is a multiset of two-element subsets of V. Two copies of {u,v} represent parallel edges joining u and v; the graph is simple exactly when each edge multiplicity is at most one.
```

## `form.wave2.graph42.simple-undirected`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.graph42.simple-undirected` — simple undirected graph (loopless). Set-based undirected graph with edges as two-element vertex subsets, no loops or parallel edges, finite and infinite V allowed

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
A simple undirected graph is a pair G=(V,E), where V is a set of vertices and E⊆{e⊆V:|e|=2}. Thus each edge {u,v} has distinct vertices u≠v, has no direction, and occurs at most once.
```

## `form.wave2.integer59.fraction-field-integral-domain`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.integer59.fraction-field-integral-domain` — fraction field of an integral domain. Integral-domain fraction field: concrete fraction-equivalence construction

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
For a commutative integral domain D, the fraction field Frac(D) is constructed from pairs (a,b) with a∈D and 0≠b∈D, where (a,b)∼(c,d) iff ad=bc. The class of (a,b) is written a/b; addition and multiplication are defined on such classes so that D embeds into the resulting field.
```

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.integer59.fraction-field-integral-domain` — fraction field of an integral domain. Integral-domain fraction field: concrete fraction-equivalence construction

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
Equivalently, a fraction field of an integral domain D is a field F with an injective ring map i:D→F satisfying: for every field K and injective ring map j:D→K, there exists a unique field homomorphism h:F→K for which h∘i=j.
```

## `form.wave2.integer59.integer-equivalence-class`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.integer59.integer-equivalence-class` — integer. Integer: natural-pair difference classes

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
An integer is an equivalence class [a,b] of pairs of natural numbers, where (a,b)∼(c,d) exactly when a+d=b+c. The integers may be realized as the quotient set (N×N)/∼, with [a,b] understood as the formal difference of a and b.
```

## `form.wave2.module94.unital-left-module`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.module94.unital-left-module` — module over a ring. Unital left R-module over a ring with identity

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
Let R be a ring with identity 1 and let (M,+) be an abelian group. A unital left R-module is an action R×M→M satisfying r(m+n)=rm+rn, (r+s)m=rm+sm, (rs)m=r(sm), and 1m=m for all r,s∈R and m,n∈M.
```

## `form.wave2.pop140.independent-pair`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.pop140.independent-pair` — independent events. Probability theory: two measurable events in a probability space, including probability-zero cases

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
For measurable A,B in a probability space (Ω,Σ,P), the events A and B are independent if and only if P(A∩B)=P(A)P(B). No positive-probability assumption is required for this product-rule definition.
```

## `form.wave2.pop140.pairwise-independent`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.pop140.pairwise-event-independence` — pairwise independent events. Probability theory: pairwise but not necessarily jointly independent indexed measurable events

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
Let (Aᵢ)_{i∈I} be measurable events on one probability space. The family is pairwise independent if for every i,j∈I with i≠j, P(Aᵢ∩Aⱼ)=P(Aᵢ)P(Aⱼ). This does not in general imply joint independence for three or more events.
```

```text
Let X be a set. A function μ*:𝒫(X)→[0,+∞] is an outer measure on X when μ*(∅)=0, A⊆B implies μ*(A)≤μ*(B), and μ*(⋃_{n=1}^∞ Aₙ)≤∑_{n=1}^∞ μ*(Aₙ) for every sequence (Aₙ) of subsets of X.
```

## `form.wave2.pop142.bounded-linear`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.pop142.bounded-linear` — bounded linear transformation. Functional analysis: linear map X→Y between real or complex normed spaces with one global bound

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
Let X,Y be normed vector spaces over the same field F∈{ℝ,ℂ} and T:X→Y a linear map. T is bounded if there exists a real M≥0 such that ||Tx||_Y≤M||x||_X for every x∈X.
```

## `form.wave2.real45.complete-ordered-real-field`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.real45.complete-ordered-real-field` — real number system (complete ordered field). Axiomatic real arithmetic: totally ordered commutative field complete for nonempty upper-bounded subsets

**Full stored formulation:**

```text

A (lower) Dedekind cut of the rational numbers is a subset A of Q such that A is nonempty, A is not all of Q, if a belongs to A then every rational q<a also belongs to A, and A has no largest element.
```

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.research81.martingale-continuous-index` — martingale. Real-valued martingale indexed by continuous time t≥0; no assumption of continuous sample paths

```text
A real-valued process (X_t)_{t≥0} adapted to a filtration (F_t)_{t≥0}, with E|X_t|<∞ for every t≥0, is a martingale if E[X_t|F_s]=X_s almost surely whenever 0≤s≤t.
```

## `form.wave2.research81.martingale-discrete.01`

**Meaning:** `meaning.wave2.research81.martingale-discrete` — martingale. Filtered probability space, integrable adapted real-valued discrete-time process indexed by nonnegative integers

**Full stored formulation:**

```text
A real-valued discrete-time process (X_n)_{n≥0}, adapted to a filtration (F_n)_{n≥0} and satisfying E|X_n|<∞ for every n, is a martingale if E[X_{n+1}|F_n]=X_n almost surely for each n≥0.
```
